## Follow-up: concrete A18/A06 teaching-review cases

- Date: 2026-06-28
- Agent ID: A06
- Workstream: Visualization Lab / MAIS Manim v2 source architecture
- Status: Completed for A06 concrete teaching-review evidence; A18 final human/curriculum signoff and A22 clean build remain open
- Objective: Move beyond global teaching-quality checks by naming concrete representative Manim scenes, objectives, cognitive-load notes, reviewer prompts, and source evidence for A18 final review.

- What changed:
  - Added 12 concrete teaching-review cases across primary, middle-school, secondary, and advanced math scenes.
  - Each case records a learning objective, math focus, visual model, cognitive-load note, reviewer prompt, source-contract evidence, semantic binding count, focused beat count, and camera shot count.
  - The cases are A06-confirmed `a06-confirmed-ready-for-a18-signoff`, with `a18FinalHumanReviewRequired: true` so this does not overclaim A18 final acceptance.
  - Folded the new teaching-review case files into the executable Manim review-package matrix under the `evidence` package.
- Concrete cases:
  - `number-line-core` / `mais-manim-number-line`: primary number-line value, unit interval, and comparison distance.
  - `fraction-slices-core` / `mais-manim-fraction-slices`: primary whole-part denominator and unit-fraction model.
  - `angle-geometry-core` / `mais-manim-angle-geometry`: middle-school angle measure as ray rotation.
  - `function-graph-core` / `mais-manim-function-graph`: secondary function rule and graph point.
  - `function-family-core` / `mais-manim-function-family`: secondary parameter changes and compared curves.
  - `trig-unit-wave-core` / `mais-manim-trig-unit-wave`: secondary unit-circle motion to sine/cosine wave.
  - `calculus-rate-area-core` / `mais-manim-calculus-rate-area`: advanced local rate versus accumulated area.
  - `conic-section-core` / `mais-manim-conic-sections-deep`: advanced plane slice through a cone.
  - `space-vectors-core` / `mais-manim-space-vectors-lines-planes`: advanced vectors, lines, and planes in 3D.
  - `probability-machine-core` / `mais-manim-probability-machine`: secondary repeated trials and empirical probability.
  - `statistics-distribution-core` / `mais-manim-statistics-distribution`: secondary center and spread.
  - `statistical-inference-core` / `mais-manim-statistical-inference-lab`: advanced sample-based interval uncertainty.
